# SESSION 03 Task Queue

Scope: 后端 Service、UseCase、Domain、Manager、Processor、Scheduler 业务逻辑、状态流转、业务校验、幂等、并发、事务边界、业务异常、重复业务规则和无意义包装层。

Generated from static traversal of `84` production Go files under `server/`, plus architecture docs and selected service/domain source reads. Formal modification is gated until this queue contains at least 1000 concrete tasks.
